Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any spectrum module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 463. The projective resolution has length at most 267 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on spectrum structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 463. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 267. QED.

By induction on n, assuming the bound 65, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 35. QED.
